# Graph Report - MythosMUD  (2026-08-17)

## Corpus Check
- 533 files · ~2,837,634 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 53149 nodes · 100330 edges · 2336 communities (1634 shown, 702 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 93% EXTRACTED · 7% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 7264 edges (avg confidence: 0.54)
- Token cost: 964,898 input · 37,188 output
